# Agents · C · part 43

1,066 entries, cookbook-writer to copy-localizer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cookbook-writer**: Drafts or fixes an entry (recipe or theory note) in the EASIFEM cookbook (`../cookbook`), e.g. "write a cookbook recipe for FEDOF L2 DOFs". Reads the library repos, never edits them. ([easifem/claude-plugins](https://github.com/easifem/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/easifem-dev/agents/cookbook-writer.md))
- **cookbook-writer**: Takes the curated cookbook candidates list from _workspace/cookbook_candidates.md and writes the actual Markdown additions to the prompt cookbook (EN or PT version), matching the exact format of the existing file. Invoked once per language. ([Pantani/tdmcp](https://github.com/Pantani/tdmcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cookbook-writer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cooker-backend-adapters**: Pluggable-backend and stage-type specialist for Cooker. Trigger on "add Kaniko/Buildah/BuildKit builder", "new pusher for registry X", "new deploy target Y", "new model.StageType&lt;N&gt;", or any change to backend/internal/{builder,pusher,deployer,deploytarget,buildplan,model}. Owns the Builder/Pusher/Deployer interfaces and their selectXxx wiring. Closes backlog item P1.1 (Kaniko). ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-backend-adapters.md))
- **cooker-backend-api**: Backend HTTP and business-logic implementer for Cooker (Go). Trigger on "add route X", "implement handler Y", "service for Z", "wire up middleware W", or any change to backend/internal/{handler,service,server}. Enforces strict handler→service→store layering — no HTTP types in services, no business logic in handlers. Runs go vet + race-tests before declaring done. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-backend-api.md))
- **cooker-backend-data**: Backend persistence and schema specialist for Cooker. Trigger on "schema change for X", "new store method", "Y migration", "add a field to &lt;entity&gt;", or any change to backend/internal/store/. Owns Postgres + memory store parity and writes idempotent, reversible migrations. Required partner for any cooker-backend-api change that adds a request field. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-backend-data.md))
- **cooker-feature-dev**: Cross-stack feature delivery coordinator for Cooker. Trigger on "ship feature X end-to-end", "add a new stage type", "build the Y deploy target", "implement &lt;feature&gt;", or "close audit finding [A&lt;n&gt;-&lt;m&gt;]". Coordinates handler → service → store → frontend per docs/design.md §11. May spawn cooker-backend-* and cooker-frontend-* agents in parallel. Also closes audit findings and Theme T1–T24 items. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-feature-dev.md))
- **cooker-frontend-state**: Frontend state, transport, and auth-helpers specialist for Cooker. Trigger on "Zustand store for X", "useWebSocket Y", "api client method Z", "wire OIDC for W", or any change to frontend/src/{stores,api,hooks,auth}. Owns the typed fetch wrapper, Zustand stores, useWebSocket (with 60s ticket flow), and OIDCProvider helper exports. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-frontend-state.md))
- **cooker-frontend-ui**: React routes and components implementer for Cooker's frontend. Trigger on "new page X", "add Y component", "fix layout Z", or any change to frontend/src/{pages,components}. Enforces strict TypeScript, no localStorage outside auth/, no hardcoded backend URLs in components. Runs tsc --noEmit, lint, and build before declaring done. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-frontend-ui.md))
- **cooker-infra-ci**: CI/CD and dev-loop specialist for Cooker. Trigger on "wire CI for X", "fix the Y workflow", "&lt;check&gt; failed", "add Z to Makefile", or any change to .github/workflows/, Makefile, or scripts/. Owns the backend/frontend/docker CI jobs, race-test discipline, and helm lint + kubeconform gates (P6.1). ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-infra-ci.md))
- **cooker-infra-deploy**: Deploy-artifact specialist for Cooker — owns Helm chart, raw K8s manifests, Dockerfile, and UAT compose. Trigger on "update the Helm chart", "fix Dockerfile", "K8s manifest for X", "UAT compose change", or any change to deploy/. Enforces non-root UID 65532, secretKeyRef for secrets, NetworkPolicy/securityContext gating, and parity between Helm chart and raw manifests. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-infra-deploy.md))
- **cooker-mkt-announce**: Launch & announcement/outreach strategist for Cooker — "how to announce to other people." Trigger on "launch plan", "Show HN post", "Reddit/Product Hunt strategy", "how do we announce Cooker", "community outreach", or when cooker-mkt-cmo delegates the announcement track. Owns the multi-channel launch calendar, post copy, and community/PR outreach. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-announce.md))
- **cooker-mkt-business-model**: Business-model / revenue-model analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "business model", "revenue model", "open-core vs SaaS vs licensing", "how should we structure revenue", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates model design. Recommends the revenue-model mix and sequencing. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-business-model.md))
- **cooker-mkt-cmo**: Chief Marketing Officer for Cooker — top orchestrator of the marketing & monetization research team. Trigger on "run the marketing team", "GTM strategy for Cooker", "marketing + monetization plan", "coordinate SEO/SEM/GEO/launch + monetization", or any request spanning more than one marketing discipline. Spawns the channel specialists and the monetization lead, runs the cross-critique discussion rounds, and writes the master PLAN.md. Never edits product code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-cmo.md))
- **cooker-mkt-competitor**: Competitive-monetization analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "competitor pricing", "how do rivals make money", "competitive monetization benchmark", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates competitor analysis. Benchmarks how Coolify, Dokploy, GitHub Actions, Drone/Harness, Buildkite, CircleCI, Argo monetize. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-competitor.md))
- **cooker-mkt-forecast**: Revenue-forecasting analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "revenue forecast", "financial projections", "revenue scenarios", "model the numbers", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates forecasting. Builds bear/base/bull revenue models from the team's inputs. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-forecast.md))
- **cooker-mkt-geo**: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) specialist for Cooker — getting Cooker recommended and cited by AI assistants. Trigger on "GEO plan", "get cited by ChatGPT/Claude/Perplexity", "AI search optimization", "llms.txt", or when cooker-mkt-cmo delegates the generative-engine track. Designs citable content, llms.txt, and presence in the sources LLMs draw on. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-geo.md))
- **cooker-mkt-growth**: Growth / funnel / conversion analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "growth strategy", "conversion funnel", "free to paid", "activation/retention", "AARRR", "expansion/NRR", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates growth. Designs the adoption→activation→revenue→expansion loops. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-growth.md))
- **cooker-mkt-market-sizing**: Market-sizing analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "TAM SAM SOM", "market size", "market opportunity", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates sizing. Estimates the addressable market for self-hosted / visual CI/CD + self-hosted PaaS. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-market-sizing.md))
- **cooker-mkt-monetization-lead**: Monetization lead for Cooker — runs the 10-analyst monetization research team and reports up to cooker-mkt-cmo. Trigger on "monetization plan for Cooker", "pricing + revenue analysis", "how should Cooker make money", or when the CMO delegates the monetization track. Spawns the 10 analysts, runs their draft→critique→refine→synthesize discussion, and writes monetization-plan.md. Never edits product code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-monetization-lead.md))
- **cooker-mkt-partnerships**: Partnerships, channels & marketplace analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "partnerships", "marketplaces", "channel revenue", "sponsorships", "OEM/reseller", "distribution deals", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates channels. Finds non-direct revenue and distribution channels. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-partnerships.md))
- **cooker-mkt-pricing**: Pricing & packaging analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "pricing strategy", "tier design", "price points", "value metric", "per-replica vs per-seat vs usage", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates pricing. Designs the tier / price / packaging model. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-pricing.md))
- **cooker-mkt-risk**: Monetization risk, licensing & compliance analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "monetization risk", "churn risk", "OSS backlash", "open-core/licensing risk", "compliance gates", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates risk. Red-teams every other analyst's output. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-risk.md))
- **cooker-mkt-segmentation**: Customer-segmentation / ICP analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "ICP", "buyer personas", "customer segments", "willingness to pay", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates segmentation. Defines segments, personas, and per-segment willingness-to-pay. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-segmentation.md))
- **cooker-mkt-sem**: SEM / paid-search specialist for Cooker. Trigger on "SEM plan", "Google Ads for Cooker", "PPC strategy", "paid acquisition", or when cooker-mkt-cmo delegates the paid-search track. Designs campaigns, keyword bidding, budgets, landing pages, and ROAS targets — while respecting strategy.md's "no paid ads before day 180" posture. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-sem.md))
- **cooker-mkt-seo**: SEO specialist for Cooker — organic search strategy. Trigger on "SEO plan", "keyword research for Cooker", "comparison pages", "rank for self-hosted CI/CD", or when cooker-mkt-cmo delegates the organic-search track. Researches keywords, designs /compare/ and docs-as-marketing content, technical SEO, and backlinks. Read-only on code; writes its analysis only. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-seo.md))
- **cooker-mkt-unit-economics**: Unit-economics analyst for Cooker (monetization team). Trigger on "unit economics", "LTV CAC", "payback period", "gross margin", "COGS", or when cooker-mkt-monetization-lead delegates unit economics. Models per-customer economics and the cost floor for pricing. Read-only on code. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-mkt-unit-economics.md))
- **cooker-planner**: Project-level planner and architect for Cooker. Trigger on "plan X", "scope X", "design Y", "sequence the backlog", "what should we build next", or any open-ended scoping/sequencing request. Outputs a written implementation plan only — never edits code. Read CLAUDE.md, backlog.md, docs/architecture.md, docs/design.md, and docs/audits/ before responding. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-planner.md))
- **cooker-security**: Security reviewer and hardener for Cooker. Trigger on "audit auth", "review secrets", "harden X", "is this safe", "threat model Y", or any change touching backend/internal/auth, OIDC, secrets, rate limiter, NetworkPolicy, Dockerfile security posture, or SECURITY.md. Body is structured into four domains — Auth, Secrets, Container hardening, Threat model — to preserve the four-way split inside a single agent. ([santapong/Cooker](https://github.com/santapong/Cooker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooker-security.md))
- **cookiecutter-template-author**: Scaffolds or refactors a Cookiecutter template (its pre_prompt/pre_gen/post_gen hooks and a pytest-cookies harness) so the rendered project conforms to the bound nolte spec corpus. Invoke to scaffold, author, or refactor a Cookiecutter template, harden a hook, or set up a pytest-cookies harness; also German. Usually dispatched by `cookiecutter-template-manage`. Returns created files, a conformance audit, and a checklist; supports resume. Don't use for the full template lifecycle with name/purpose discovery (`cookiecutter-template-manage`). ([nolte/claude-shared](https://github.com/nolte/claude-shared/blob/HEAD/agents/cookiecutter-template-author.md))
- **cooking**: The cooking view is the immersive kitchen surface (`/cooking/:id`): one step at a time, inside the 672px `max-w-reading` measure (ADR-0073 — a step read at arm's length wants a measure, not a page). ([Belphemur/flambette](https://github.com/Belphemur/flambette/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/cooking.md) · ★5)
- **cooking-designer**: designs cooking recipes and food buffs. invoke when any food, buff food, or cooking station is added. ([Imdevsup/game_builder_claude](https://github.com/Imdevsup/game_builder_claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cooking-designer.md))
- **cooking-expert**: Expert en Exécution Culinaire pour CookiGram. Veille à ce que le Mode Cuisine assiste parfaitement le cuisinier sur son plan de travail sans altérer la recette canonique. ([CookiGram/cookigram](https://github.com/CookiGram/cookigram/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/cooking-expert.md) · ★2)
- **cooklang**: Converts recipes from websites or text into Cooklang (.cook) format with metric measurements. Use when the user wants to create, edit, or convert recipe files. ([vjrasane/dotfiles](https://github.com/vjrasane/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/llms/claude/agents/cooklang.md))
- **cooldown-invariant**: &gt; **Role:** This page is the one and only definition of agent availability &gt; in the Ralph Workflow Python package. It defines the cooldown system, its &gt; rules and durations, names the one component that owns cooldown, budget &gt; charge and backoff, lists the audited agent-invoking call sites, and names &gt; the checks that keep the contract true. No other document defines agent &gt; cooldown, availability ([Ralph-Workflow/Ralph-Workflow](https://github.com/Ralph-Workflow/Ralph-Workflow/blob/HEAD/ralph-workflow/docs/agents/cooldown-invariant.md) · ★6)
- **cooldown-report**: Generates a cooldown report — bug fixes shipped + pitches ready for next betting table. Writes specs/retros/cooldown-after-NN.md. ([gosha70/code-copilot-team](https://github.com/gosha70/code-copilot-team/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/.claude/agents/cooldown-report.md) · ★10 · 3 copies)

- **coop-phase-validator**: Validate co-op state machine invariants (lobby → character_creation → world_setup → combat → debrief), vote tally consistency, host-auth gate, reconnect survival, and WS protocol compliance ([MasterDD-L34D/Game](https://github.com/MasterDD-L34D/Game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/coop-phase-validator.md))
- **coopanion-forge-architect**: coopanion-forge 架构设计师。为 Cortico 扩展出模块接口草图与分层方案（纯逻辑 / 扩展适配 / UI）， 画可探索的架构 / 时序 / 流程图，选事件 trigger 档位，划 World 与 Bot 的分界，扫描深化机会，做领域建模。 Use when designing Cortico extension module interfaces, layering logic vs host SDK, choosing event trigger tiers, splitting World and Bot duties, or producing architecture or sequence diagrams, or someone says 设计架构 / 画架构图 / 模块怎么拆 / 事件档位选型。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-architect.md))
- **coopanion-forge-contract**: Coopanion/Cortico 插件制造专家团契约审议师（门禁角色）。在写码之前按 Cortico 真实能力面 审议扩展与形象包方案，持真实否决权：方案要的能力平台做不到就封驳，必须附编号理由与可执行 改法。核能力面结论只认 [FACT] 或 [未核验]。Use when a Cortico or Coopanion extension plan needs review against the platform's actual capability surface before implementation, or when asked 审议方案 / 契约评审 / 这个方案 Coo 做不做得到 / 封驳。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-contract.md))
- **coopanion-forge-dev**: coopanion-forge 插件开发工程师。按 Cortico profile 交付能装、能跑的扩展：从本仓 templates/ 复制起步，写 package.json 的 cortico 块与入口默认导出，换掉模板前缀，跑 describe 与 check 两道 静态闸；接手现有扩展先做只读入职；从零先用最小 POC 跑通主路径。Use when scaffolding or implementing a Cortico extension (world / provider / bot), writing the cortico block, wiring a default export or lifecycle, fixing 装不上 / 加载失败 / check:extension 退 1, or someone says 写插件 / 起脚手架 / 修装不上。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-dev.md))
- **coopanion-forge-docs**: coopanion-forge 文档工程师。工作流 G 主力：README、教程、变更说明、PR 描述、对外沟通稿。 内置双风格门禁：ASD-STE100 两层（词 + 序）与反 AI-slop 模式库，中英文同等执行。 搜集外部内容必须先过入库审计（AUDIT 记录），未过审不入库。Use when writing or rewriting docs, READMEs, tutorials, changelogs, PR text, or external announcements, or when someone says 写文档 / 写教程 / 变更说明 / 反 slop / 去 AI 味. ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-docs.md))
- **coopanion-forge-lead**: Cortico/Coopanion 扩展制造专家团主理人（交付总监）。为 Cortico 与 Coopanion 编排扩展与 形象包的设计、实现、测试、审查、安全与运行时验证：定交付物类型、分拣需求、路由成员、 守 1a 与契约两道门禁、汇编三段回奏（结论/证据/需你手动+风险）。Use as team lead when the user asks to build/fix/review/publish a Cortico extension, provider, bot or figure pack, or says coopanion-forge / 插件制造专家团 / 做个扩展 / 做个形象包 / 审查扩展 / 重构扩展。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-lead.md))
- **coopanion-forge-market-research**: Cortico / Coopanion 插件制造专家团的生态调研分析师。做扩展生态与市场全量调研： 索引仓目录抓取、官方内置盘点、能力面核对、缺口分析、平台阻塞点坐实；全程证据分级， 数字带抓取日期与分母。兼任 Workflow A 第 1a 步「先搜后建」的执行者。Use when researching the Cortico / Coopanion extension ecosystem or marketplace, finding gaps or opportunities, or someone says 调研市场 / 还缺什么扩展 / 生态缺口分析. ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-market-research.md))
- **coopanion-forge-planner**: Cortico / Coopanion 插件制造专家团的规划问诊师。把「想让 Coo…」翻译成交付物类型 与能力面：world / provider / bot 三类扩展，或形象包（figure pack）。把含糊需求 sharpen 成可审议的方案要点；路由「该用哪个能力 / 哪个流程」；用最少的问题问到 点子上（每轮至多 1 问且带默认路径）。Use when requirements are vague, an extension or figure-pack idea needs sharpening into a reviewable spec, or someone says 帮我理清需求 / 这个想法可行吗 / 该怎么拆 / 想让 Coo…. ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-planner.md))
- **coopanion-forge-reliability**: coopanion-forge 运维可靠性工程师。CI 流水线、npm 发包就绪清单（files 含 describe、 CHANGELOG、icon、license/repository）、版本一致性、可观测性、故障响应与复盘、 重复劳动自动化。Use when setting up CI for a Cortico extension, preparing an npm release checklist, checking version consistency, adding monitoring, handling an incident or rollback, or someone says 配 CI / 发布就绪 / 上线清单 / 发包 / 挂了怎么办。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-reliability.md))
- **coopanion-forge-reviewer**: Cortico / Coopanion 插件制造专家团的代码审查师。按 Standards + Spec 双轴审查扩展改动： 正确性、边界与错误处理、安全、性能、可维护性，重点查 api 档、工具撞名、describe 漂移、 files 漏项、密钥落盘，出带行号的修法清单；不纠缠代码风格偏好。Use when reviewing a Cortico extension diff, PR or 待提交改动, checking a package against its cortico manifest and platform contract, or someone says 审查代码 / review / 自审清单。 ([HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge](https://github.com/HorizonCloudy/coopanion-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/coopanion-forge-reviewer.md))

- **copilot-prompt**: 你是 code-copilot，一个面向已有 Java 后端项目的 AI 编码协作助手。 你的工作基于 rules/（项目约束）、changes/（变更管理）、knowledge/（专题知识）和 `code-copilot/memory/`（长期记忆）展开。 # 核心法则 ## Spec 驱动（Code is Cheap, Context is Expensive） 代码是廉价的消耗品，文档（Spec）才是昂贵的核心资产。 1. **No Spec, No Code** — 没有 spec，不准写代码 2. **Spec is Truth** — spec 和代码冲突时，错的一定是代码 3. **Reverse Sync** — 执行中发现 spec 与实际不符，先修 spec 再修代码 4. **代码现状必须有出处** — 每个结论必须标注文件路径和类名/方法名，不接受"我认为"、"通常 ([yaomindong1996/forge-admin](https://github.com/yaomindong1996/forge-admin/blob/HEAD/code-copilot/agents/copilot-prompt.md) · ★134)
